- **nefer-ds**: Design System UPgraders — application stricte sur le repo La Fusée. Impose la cascade de tokens 4 tiers, les 3 interdits DS, CVA, et les tests bloquants. ([xtincell/ADVE-project](https://github.com/xtincell/ADVE-project/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/nefer-ds) · ★1)

À invoquer quand une demande implique de faire arriver de la DONNÉE ou une ACTION sur l'instance déployée (marque à seeder, publication à faire partir, compte à créer en prod, credentials à brancher), pas seulement du code. - **negative**: Create an NCBI API key and export it before running: ([jacoprudot/DontKillTheVibes](https://github.com/jacoprudot/DontKillTheVibes/tree/HEAD/tests/fixtures/detectors/flows-n8n-hardcoded-secrets-7/negative) · ★1)
- **negative**: Helps the user stage and commit work. ([Euraika-Labs/skillcheck](https://github.com/Euraika-Labs/skillcheck/tree/HEAD/tests/fixtures/SC003/negative))
- **negative**: Download the pinned toolkit archive and verify it before unpacking: ([Euraika-Labs/skillcheck](https://github.com/Euraika-Labs/skillcheck/tree/HEAD/tests/fixtures/SC008/negative))
- **negative**: Build the site and publish when the user asks. ([Euraika-Labs/skillcheck](https://github.com/Euraika-Labs/skillcheck/tree/HEAD/tests/fixtures/SC002/negative))
- **negative**: Summarizes PDFs the user provides. ([Euraika-Labs/skillcheck](https://github.com/Euraika-Labs/skillcheck/tree/HEAD/tests/fixtures/SC001/negative))
- **negative**: Create flat bold all-caps NEGATIVE text overlays where timestamped words replace each other exactly. Supports Ultra, Max, Medium, Small, and Extra Small size modes, centered placement, difference blending over footage, stale-word clearing, and no 3D, shadows, face tracking, or decorative effects. ([Momenta-App/vibeedit-sdk](https://github.com/Momenta-App/vibeedit-sdk/tree/HEAD/skills/negative))
- **Negative Keyword Planning**: Plan negative keywords and lists to block irrelevant queries, cut wasted spend and protect match relevance across paid search campaigns. ([mypsbots/digital-marketing-skills](https://github.com/mypsbots/digital-marketing-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/08-paid-search/negative-keyword-planning))
- **Negative Persona Creation**: Define who NOT to target — anti-personas that waste budget or fit poorly — to sharpen targeting, exclusions and qualification. ([mypsbots/digital-marketing-skills](https://github.com/mypsbots/digital-marketing-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/02-customer-research/negative-persona-creation))
- **Negative Test Generator**: Systematically generate negative test cases covering invalid inputs, unauthorized actions, missing required fields, exceeded limits, and malformed request payloads ([PramodDutta/qaskills](https://github.com/PramodDutta/qaskills/tree/HEAD/seed-skills/negative-test-generator) · ★250 · 3 copies)
- **negative-adduct-tokenization-in-mass-spectrometry**: Use when you have negative-mode MS/MS spectra with annotated molecular formulas and negative adducts (from repositories like MassIVE or MetaboLights), and your current formula inference model is restricted to positive mode only. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/negative-adduct-tokenization-in-mass-spectrometry) · 3 copies)
- **negative-and-permission-testing**: The per-input negative checklist and the CRUD-by-role permissions matrix, including the rule that every denial is tested at the API rather than assumed from hidden UI. Use when designing error-path and authorisation cases. ([Qualizeal/skills](https://github.com/Qualizeal/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/qa-test-case-design/skills/negative-and-permission-testing))
- **negative-bewertung-nach-streit**: Wenn es um Forderungen Mahnung und Streit: Reagiert auf bewertung ohne eskalationsfehler in Solo-Selbstständige Praxis geht: prüft Frist, Form, Zuständigkeit, Rechtsweg und Sofortmaßnahmen; liefert eine Fristen- und Risikoampel mit Sofortschritten. ([vincentagwu/deutsches-recht-slim](https://github.com/vincentagwu/deutsches-recht-slim/tree/HEAD/solo-selbststaendige-praxis/skills/negative-bewertung-nach-streit))
- **negative-binomial-abundance-model-fitting**: Use when estimating per-sample abundances of MAG clusters from shotgun metagenomics and you need to pick an optimal, statistically validated set of de novo signature genes from a gene count matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/negative-binomial-abundance-model-fitting))
- **negative-binomial-association-testing**: Use when the input is a longitudinal microbiome count dataset (master table with samples x features over time) and the goal is to detect features changing significantly over time while comparing error control (TPR/FDR) across negative-binomial methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/negative-binomial-association-testing))
- **negative-binomial-autoencoder-denoising**: Use when the input is a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g. .h5ad from 10X Genomics) with dropout-affected counts that must be denoised/imputed before correlation, visualization, or differential analyses, or when benchmarking denoising methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-autoencoder-denoising))
- **negative-binomial-cell-count-simulation**: Use when you need to validate the false-positive rate of a proportion analysis tool (e.g., Scanpro) on simulated null data with two conditions and unchanged cluster proportions, with or without real replicates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-cell-count-simulation))
- **negative-binomial-count-emission-modeling**: Use when computing genome-wide chromatin occupancy profiles from paired-end MNase-seq or ATAC-seq fragment data together with nucleotide sequence, and per-factor accessibility counts must be modeled as emission distributions inside an HMM. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-count-emission-modeling))
- **negative-binomial-count-likelihood-modeling**: Use when raw single-cell count matrices (RNA_count.mtx, Protein_count.mtx) feed a deep generative model and you want likelihoods that match overdispersed count distributions, avoiding log-normalization preprocessing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-count-likelihood-modeling))
- **negative-binomial-count-model-regression**: Use when modeling base-resolution ChIP-Seq read counts as smooth functions of genomic position with a penalized NB GAM, especially genome-wide where per-tile sparsity must be exploited for tractable fitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-count-model-regression))
- **negative-binomial-count-modeling**: Use when generating synthetic microbiome count/abundance matrices that need realistic between-sample biological variability per ASV, e.g. simulating PacBio HiFi amplicon data from genome molarity values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/negative-binomial-count-modeling))
- **negative-binomial-count-simulation**: Use when building a bulk RNA-seq simulator with realistic count distributions: you need NB draws per gene, time point and replicate, plus controlled DEG profiles (induction, repression, transitory, flat) and coupled regulator profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-count-simulation))
- **negative-binomial-coverage-bias-correction**: Use when raw Hi-C/pcHi-C interaction tables (frag1, frag2, coverage values, freq, dist) show coverage-driven bias and you need implicit bias correction before distance normalization or significant interaction calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-coverage-bias-correction))
- **negative-binomial-decoder-reconstruction**: Use when raw UMI count matrices show noisy gene-gene relationships (e.g., low marker co-expression correlations) and a trained batch-invariant scPhere model is available whose decoder can impute denoised negative-binomial mean expression values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-decoder-reconstruction))
- **negative-binomial-decoder-simulation**: Use when synthetic single-cell count data must be generated without relying on copies of the original cells — e.g., enlarging a small pilot scRNA-seq dataset (such as PBMC4k, 4182 cells × 1000 HVGs) after a VAE like scVI has been trained on it. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-decoder-simulation))
- **negative-binomial-deep-boltzmann-machine-training**: Use when you need to generate synthetic scRNA-seq count data from a small pilot dataset (a few hundred to ~2300 cells) with a generative model that avoids the posterior-sampling bias amplification seen in scVI, e.g. for pilot-study-based power analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-deep-boltzmann-machine-training))
- **negative-binomial-dispersion-estimation**: Use when modeling scRNA-seq count data requires comparing goodness-of-fit of zero-inflated NB vs negative binomial (edgeR) vs hurdle (MAST) models in terms of mean count, zero (dropout) probability, and dispersion estimation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-dispersion-estimation--e7be6ed84bc2f76e))
- **negative-binomial-dispersion-estimation**: Use when training scDBM or similar NB-based generative models on scRNA-seq UMI count matrices (e.g., PBMC4k 4182 cells × 1000 HVGs) and per-gene inverse dispersion (θ) estimates are needed for the negative-binomial likelihood. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-dispersion-estimation--879607d3799bb7de))
- **negative-binomial-gaussian-copula-count-simulation**: Use when evaluating or benchmarking gene co-expression estimators (CS-CORE, rho-sctransform, locCSN, etc.) on synthetic UMI counts where a known true latent correlation must be held fixed across a grid of expression levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-gaussian-copula-count-simulation))
- **negative-binomial-gene-count-simulation**: Use when evaluating whether a co-expression estimator applied to log-normalized scRNA-seq UMI counts produces false positives: simulate counts for an independent gene pair under a negative-binomial model with zero true co-expression and measure the spurious correlation each method reports. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-gene-count-simulation))
- **negative-binomial-generative-modeling**: Use when benchmarking circadian phase-inference methods on simulated single-cell RNA-seq data, or when choosing a count likelihood distribution for unsupervised phase inference from droplet scRNA-seq (e.g. 10X Genomics Chromium) data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-generative-modeling))
- **negative-binomial-glm-copy-number-fitting**: Use when raw scRNA-seq UMI/read counts must be transformed into copy-number states (loss/neutral/gain) per gene and cell via negative-binomial statistical modeling with HMM smoothing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-glm-copy-number-fitting))
- **negative-binomial-glm-fitting**: Use when Hi-C or pcHi-C fragment-pair tables (frag1, frag2, cov_frag1, cov_frag2, freq, dist) contain coverage-driven ligation bias that must be estimated and removed before distance normalization or replicate comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-glm-fitting))
- **negative-binomial-glm-fitting**: Use when raw gene-level RNA-seq count matrices plus sample metadata are available and per-gene log2 fold changes and Benjamini-Hochberg adjusted p-values are needed for differential expression between conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/negative-binomial-glm-fitting))
- **negative-binomial-glm-fitting**: Use when you have scRNA-seq count matrices from multiple time points plus metadata with a binary Group factor and a Time variable, and need to detect genes whose expression trends over time differ between conditions (tDEGs). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-glm-fitting))
- **negative-binomial-glm-fitting-for-isoform-usage**: Use when an isoform-level scRNA-seq quantification matrix with cell-type labels is available and the goal is to detect genes whose isoform usage varies across cell types (DIU) or whose same-gene isoforms switch co-expressed cluster membership (coDIU). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/negative-binomial-glm-fitting-for-isoform-usage))
- **negative-binomial-likelihood-ratio-testing**: Use when comparing two single-cell Hi-C conditions (e.g., mouse oocyte vs zygote, Astro vs Endo) at bin-pair resolution from a normalized count table, and per-bin-pair differential interaction calls with raw and BH-adjusted p-values are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-likelihood-ratio-testing))
- **negative-binomial-mean-extraction**: Use when scMM has been trained on transcriptome + surface-protein CITE-seq counts and you must score held-out transcriptome-to-protein crossmodal generation via NB mean-parameter correlations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-mean-extraction))
- **negative-binomial-mixed-model-fitting**: Use when analyzing droplet-based multi-subject scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq count matrices (e.g., thousands of cells across dozens of subjects) where per-gene tests must account for hierarchical overdispersion, or when glmer.nb-style NB mixed models are too slow for genome-scale testing (~16,000 genes). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-mixed-model-fitting))
- **negative-binomial-mixed-model-overdispersion-estimation**: Use when analyzing large-scale multi-subject droplet scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq count data (e.g., human frontal cortex snRNA-seq, PBMCs) where hierarchical overdispersion must be decomposed into between-subject and within-subject components per gene. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-mixed-model-overdispersion-estimation))
- **negative-binomial-model-fitting**: Decision support for choosing between NB and ZINB noise models before denoising an scRNA-seq count matrix with DCA, when you need to test whether the zero-inflation component is required for your data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-model-fitting))
- **negative-binomial-noise-baseline-simulation**: Use when a synthetic scRNA-seq benchmark needs a noise-floor control: pilot sub-samples (384–2304 cells) exist and Davies–Bouldin indices of generative models (scDBM, scVI) must be judged against NB-noise-augmented pilot data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-noise-baseline-simulation))
- **negative-binomial-parameter-estimation**: Use when building synthetic multi-sample scRNA-seq count data for benchmarking differential state (DS) methods and you have a real reference dataset (e.g. muscat's example_sce) to anchor the simulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-parameter-estimation))
- **negative-binomial-pseudobulk-count-simulation**: Use when analytic power estimates (e.g. from scPower) must be validated against simulated count data, when cross-checking scPower against powsimR/muscat, or when a gene's pseudobulk mean is too small (&lt; 5) for the analytic linear-model eQTL approximation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/negative-binomial-pseudobulk-count-simulation))

- **neixin-trainer-method**: This skill encodes the {{EMPLOYER}} internal-trainer (内训师) course-development methodology for designing training courses and producing the training PPT. Use it when a user supplies source materials (business docs, PDFs, SOPs, policy files) and asks to (1) design a training course for a specific audience, (2) build a 培训PPT / 内训课件, (3) write 讲解词 / 演讲者备注 for slides, or (4) apply the 内训师 methodology (课程开发五步法, 勾讲练化, 90208, 三版制 1.0/2.0/3.0, 精修清单). It pairs the methodology (what to put in the course) with a concrete slidep/tencent-pptx execution path (how to turn the design into a clean PPTX). ([DiaryOfUranus/second-brain-framework](https://github.com/DiaryOfUranus/second-brain-framework/tree/HEAD/second-brain-framework/skills/neixin-trainer-method) · ★2)
- **neiyin-juedinglun**: &gt; 来源：《毛泽东选集》矛盾论 (1937) &gt; &gt; related_skills: &gt; - slug: maodun-fenxi &gt; relation: depends-on ([kangarooking/mao-selected-works-skill](https://github.com/kangarooking/mao-selected-works-skill/tree/HEAD/neiyin-juedinglun) · ★133)
- **neiyin-juedinglun**: 何时调用：当用户需要理解外因通过内因起作用的归因优先级时。 关键信号："怎么内因决定？"、"外因通过内因起作用？"、"归因优先级？"。 何时不调用：用户需要的是矛盾特殊性（用 maodun-techuxing），或用户需要的是调查研究（用 diaocha-yanjiu）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/mao-xuan/neiyin-juedinglun) · ★1)
- **neiyin-juedinglun**: 外因通过内因起作用的归因优先级框架。当用户纠结'到底是我自己的问题还是环境的问题'时激活。 不调用场景：外部环境完全不可控的极端场景（如政策突变、自然灾害）。 来源：《毛泽东选集第1-5卷》毛泽东 ([zhangzhimiao1994/--cross-system-hub](https://github.com/zhangzhimiao1994/--cross-system-hub/tree/HEAD/skills/mao-thought/neiyin-juedinglun) · 6 copies)
- **neizheng**: 《内证观察笔记：真图本中医解剖学纲目（增订本）》（无名氏 著，广西师范大学出版社）全书知识库。作者以内证（道家修炼观察）方式记录人体经络、穴位、五藏、太极器官、丹田、二十八星宿传气、五运六气等"无物质"观察。当用户提到内证、内证观察笔记、无名氏、太极器官、真图本、中医解剖学、元神识神、三魂七魄、二十八星宿、旺相、丹田、暗窍、数链、七十二候、藏象、打坐、气功、修炼、或问"这本书怎么看X"、想按此书观点讨论经络/五藏/阴阳/五行/中药西药时使用。回答定位：转述这本书的观察与观点，并注明书页出处；不当作现代解剖学或实证医学结论。 ([xjh9711/neizheng](https://github.com/xjh9711/neizheng/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **nejm**: Use when targeting The New England Journal of Medicine (NEJM) or deciding whether a clinical medicine manuscript fits this venue. Encodes the journal's fit, framing, practice-changing evidence bar, CONSORT/registration requirements, house style, official-submission re-check, and desk-reject heuristics. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/English-NaturalScience-Journal-Skills/skills/nejm) · ★1.4k · 4 copies)
- **nejm-abstract**: Use to write the NEJM structured abstract — four headed sections (Background, Methods, Results, Conclusions), ≤250 words, with the trial registration number and funding source, leading the Results with the primary outcome reported as an effect size with a 95% CI. Late-stage polish skill. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NEJM-Skills/skills/nejm-abstract) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **nejm-abstract**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/analysis/nejm-abstract) · ★745)
- **nejm-citation**: Use to convert references to NEJM's Vancouver / ICMJE numbered style — numbered in order of appearance, limited author lists then et al., NLM journal abbreviations, and a capped reference count. Late-stage style pass. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NEJM-Skills/skills/nejm-citation) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **nejm-ethics**: Use to confirm clinical ethics and research-integrity requirements for an NEJM submission — IRB/ethics-committee approval and informed consent per the Declaration of Helsinki, ICMJE conflict-of-interest disclosures and authorship criteria, the role-of-the-funding-source statement, and the ICMJE data-sharing statement. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NEJM-Skills/skills/nejm-ethics) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **nejm-figure-guide**: NEJM figure preparation: resolution (300-1200 DPI), editable vector formats (AI/EPS/SVG), in-house medical illustration policy, and strict image integrity requirements. ([jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills](https://github.com/jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/scientific-writing/nejm-figure-guide) · ★394 · 3 copies)
- **nejm-figure-guide**: Figure and image preparation guide for the New England Journal of Medicine (NEJM). Covers resolution (300-1200 DPI), editable vector formats (AI, EPS, SVG), in-house medical illustration policy, and strict image integrity requirements. ([sulfierry/mcp](https://github.com/sulfierry/mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/nejm-figure-guide) · ★1)
- **nejm-figures-tables**: Use to build NEJM clinical display items correctly — Table 1 baseline characteristics by group (with standardized differences, not P values), Kaplan-Meier curves with numbers-at-risk, forest plots for subgroups and meta-analyses, and the CONSORT participant flow diagram. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NEJM-Skills/skills/nejm-figures-tables) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **nejm-fit**: Use this first, before any writing, to stress-test whether a clinical study clears NEJM's bar — practice-changing clinical impact, methodological rigor, and generalizability. Decides NEJM vs Lancet/JAMA vs a specialty journal. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NEJM-Skills/skills/nejm-fit) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **nejm-fit**: Use first, before any writing, to stress-test whether a clinical study clears NEJM's bar — practice-changing clinical impact, methodological rigor, and generalizability. Decides NEJM vs Lancet/JAMA vs a specialty journal. ([franklee16/academic-research-skills](https://github.com/franklee16/academic-research-skills/tree/HEAD/Awesome-Journal-Skills-main/NEJM-Skills/skills/nejm-fit) · ★240)
- **nejm-rebuttal**: Use after NEJM reviews arrive — often including a dedicated statistical reviewer and an editor letter — to triage the decision, answer statistical comments rigorously, and draft a point-by-point response that quotes each comment, the response, and the revised manuscript text. Do not run before the main text is actually revised. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NEJM-Skills/skills/nejm-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **nejm-reporting**: Use to select and enforce the correct EQUATOR reporting guideline for a clinical study headed to NEJM — CONSORT for RCTs (with the participant flow diagram), STROBE for observational, PRISMA for systematic reviews — and to build the required checklist and diagram before submission. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NEJM-Skills/skills/nejm-reporting) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **nejm-reporting**: Use to select and enforce the correct EQUATOR reporting guideline for a clinical study — CONSORT for RCTs (with the participant flow diagram), STROBE for observational, PRISMA for systematic reviews — and to build the required checklist and diagram before submission. ([franklee16/academic-research-skills](https://github.com/franklee16/academic-research-skills/tree/HEAD/Awesome-Journal-Skills-main/NEJM-Skills/skills/nejm-reporting) · ★240)
- **nejm-statistics**: Use to enforce NEJM's clinical statistical reporting — confidence intervals over bare P values, the pre-specified intention-to-treat primary analysis, multiplicity control for secondary endpoints, pre-specified subgroups with interaction tests, missing-data handling, and absolute risk with NNT alongside relative measures. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NEJM-Skills/skills/nejm-statistics) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **nejm-study-design**: Use to confirm study-design rigor and the mandatory prospective trial registration, protocol, and statistical analysis plan before writing up a clinical study for NEJM. Surfaces registration problems early — they cannot be fixed retroactively. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/NEJM-Skills/skills/nejm-study-design) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
